### [78] [Closest univariate convex linear-quadratic function approximation with minimal number of Pieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18164)
*Namrata Kundu,Yves Lucet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用最少分段数的凸分段线性二次（PLQ）函数逼近任意单变量PLQ函数，以欧氏范数度量距离，提出结合贪婪搜索预处理与二分搜索的算法，并在道路设计中简化垂直对齐曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分段线性二次函数在优化与工程中广泛应用，但现有凸逼近方法多针对样条且依赖全局最优求解，缺乏对"最少分段数凸PLQ逼近"这一问题的系统处理；同时道路设计等实际场景需要简化垂直对齐曲线。

Method: 在欧氏距离度量下：先固定输出函数的断点数量与位置，构造并求解一个凸优化问题；再固定断点数量但不固定位置，求解非凸优化问题以确定最优断点布置；最后提出由贪婪搜索预处理与二分搜索组成的算法，通过求解对数数量级的优化问题逼近任意PLQ函数，从而两步获得最少分段数的最近凸函数。使用MATLAB中YALMIP库配合CPLEX、Gurobi、BARON选择高效求解器。

Result: 通过多个数值示例验证算法；与已有的全局最优单变量样条逼近算法进行对比；并成功应用于道路设计中垂直对齐曲线的简化，验证了方法的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 所提算法能够在最少分段数条件下有效求得最近凸PLQ逼近，兼具理论保证与工程适用性，并可推广至道路设计等实际优化问题。

Abstract: We compute the closest convex piecewise linear-quadratic (PLQ) function with minimal number of pieces to a given univariate piecewise linear-quadratic function. The Euclidean norm is used to measure the distance between functions. First, we assume that the number and positions of the breakpoints of the output function are fixed, and solve a convex optimization problem. Next, we assume the number of breakpoints is fixed, but not their position, and solve a nonconvex optimization problem to determine optimal breakpoints placement. Finally, we propose an algorithm composed of a greedy search preprocessing and a dichotomic search that solves a logarithmic number of optimization problems to obtain an approximation of any PLQ function with minimal number of pieces thereby obtaining in two steps the closest convex function with minimal number of pieces.
  We illustrate our algorithms with multiple examples, compare our approach with a previous globally optimal univariate spline approximation algorithm, and apply our method to simplify vertical alignment curves in road design optimization. CPLEX, Gurobi, and BARON are used with the YALMIP library in MATLAB to effectively select the most efficient solver.

</details>


### [79] [Persistent mixed worldviews in the theory of chosen preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19316)
*Ziran Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文检验并修正了 Bernheim 等 (2021, AER) 关于"选择偏好"理论中世界观收敛的猜想：原猜想不成立，但作者给出了混合世界观持续存在的充分条件、收敛到纯世界观的充要条件，并刻画了均衡的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernheim 等 (2021) 提出选择偏好理论，并猜想在低思维灵活性下个体世界观会收敛到纯世界观。本文旨在检验该猜想的正确性，并刻画混合世界观在平稳均衡中持续或收敛的边界条件。

Method: 理论建模与均衡分析。构造含任意多世界观的共同价值行动模型；运用有限线性规划刻画持续性条件；通过估价极大值分析混合累积点；在"严格定制世界观"下证明存在性并给出一步均衡刻画，同时考察弱可实施性与平稳随机化。

Result: 原猜想失败：即使所有纯世界观都是吸收态，从相对开放的初始世界观集合出发，每个平稳均衡中混合世界观仍可持续。有限线性规划给出充分持续性条件；相反，混合累积点要求共享的估价极大值。若每个可实施行动有唯一最大化评估者，则所有内点灵活性与贴现参数下每个既有平稳均衡收敛到纯化；经验效用无唯一性时亦收敛。既有均衡中，小的支付扰动在固定期限上以高概率保持妥协。严格定制世界观可保证存在性；弱可实施性可能排除平稳纯策略存在。

Conclusion: Bernheim 等 (2021) 的收敛猜想需被修正：混合世界观在宽泛条件下可持续存在。持续性、收敛性结果可推广至平稳随机化，为选择偏好理论的均衡刻画提供了更精确的理论基础。

Abstract: Bernheim, Braghieri, Martínez-Marquina, and Zuckerman (2021, American Economic Review) develop a theory of chosen preferences and conjecture convergence to pure worldviews at low mindset flexibility. We show that the (original) conjecture fails: with arbitrarily many worldviews, a common-value action sustains mixed worldviews in every stationary equilibrium from a relatively open set of initial worldviews, even when all pure worldviews are absorbing.
  However, finite linear programs characterize a sufficient persistence condition. Conversely, mixed accumulation points require shared valuation maxima. If each implementable action has a unique maximizing evaluator, every existing stationary equilibrium converges to purity for all interior flexibility and discount parameters. Experienced utility converges without uniqueness. In existing equilibria, small payoff perturbations preserve compromise over fixed horizons with high probability. Strictly tailored worldviews yield existence and a one-step equilibrium characterization at sufficiently low mindset flexibility; weak implementation can preclude stationary pure-strategy existence. The persistence and convergence results extend to stationary randomization.

</details>


### [80] [NomadBBO: A direct-search framework for constrained mixed-variable blackbox optimization in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19333)
*Edward Hallé-Hannan,Christophe Tribes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 NomadBBO，一个基于 Nomad 的 Python 库，用于带不等式约束的混合变量黑箱优化，其核心算法 CatADS 将 ADS 扩展到分类变量并支持代理模型与混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合变量黑箱优化在仿真工程与机器学习中广泛存在，目标与约束函数评估昂贵，变量可为连续、整数、二元或分类。现有无导数优化库虽多，但很少同时兼顾易用性、计算效率、灵活性与理论收敛保证。

Method: 核心框架 CatADS 是一种新的直接搜索方法：对定量变量沿用自适应直接搜索（ADS）机制，对分类变量引入邻域机制，邻域距离采用基于高斯过程的距离或基于数据的经验 Wasserstein 距离；不等式约束通过渐进障碍（progressive barrier）处理。混合变量与代理机制用 Python 实现，并通过 Cython 连接 Nomad 的高效 C++ 后端，使其能处理分类变量，同时支持外部库与混合优化策略，包括 SMT 2.0 的高斯过程模型和贝叶斯优化。

Result: 在 Cat-Suite 基准集合的带约束与无约束混合变量问题上，将 NomadBBO 与其他求解器进行了数值实验对比（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: NomadBBO 提供了易用且高效的混合变量黑箱优化工具，兼具理论收敛保证与灵活扩展能力；beta 版本发布于 https://test.pypi.org/project/NomadBBO/。

Abstract: Mixed-variable blackbox optimization arises in simulation-based engineering and machine learning, where objective and constraint functions are expensive to evaluate and variables may be continuous, integer, binary, or categorical. Several software libraries are available for derivative-free optimization, but few combine ease of use, computational efficiency, flexibility, and theoretical convergence guarantees. This work presents NomadBBO, a user-friendly Python library for inequality-constrained mixed-variable blackbox optimization built around Nomad. Its core optimization framework is CatADS, a new direct-search method that extends Adaptive Direct Search (ADS) to mixed-variable problems. CatADS combines the mechanisms of ADS for quantitative variables with neighborhoods for handling categorical variables. These neighborhoods use Gaussian process-based or empirical Wasserstein distances derived from available data. Inequality constraints are handled through the progressive barrier. The mixed-variable and surrogate-based mechanisms are implemented in Python and connected to the efficient C++ backend of Nomad via Cython. This integration allows Nomad to handle categorical variables. It also enables the use of external libraries and hybrid optimization strategies, including Gaussian process models from SMT 2.0 and Bayesian optimization. Numerical experiments compare NomadBBO with other solvers on constrained and unconstrained mixed-variable problems from the Cat-Suite benchmark collection. The beta release is available at https://test.pypi.org/project/NomadBBO/

</details>


### [81] [Computing the convex envelope of bivariate piecewise linear-quadratic functions in linear time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19343)
*Tanmaya Karmarkar,Yves Lucet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算二元分段线性二次（PLQ）函数凸包络的实用线性时间算法，并证明凸包络为分段有理函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸的二元分段线性二次函数的凸包络在优化中具有重要意义，但此前缺乏高效的精确计算方法，且凸包络的函数形式未被明确刻画。

Method: 算法分五步：（1）对每个二次分片求凸包络，得到定义在多面体剖分上的分段有理函数（二次除以线性）；（2）对每个结果分片求Legendre-Fenchel共轭，得到定义在抛物剖分上的分段二次函数；（3）取所有函数的最大值，得到原PLQ函数共轭，表示为抛物剖分上的分段二次函数；（4）对每个分片再求共轭；（5）取最大值得到凸包络（二次共轭），表示为多面体剖分上的有理函数。

Result: 算法以线性时间运行，能够精确计算二元PLQ函数的凸包络，并证明该凸包络是定义在多面体剖分上的分段有理函数（二次除以线性）。

Conclusion: 该工作为二元PLQ函数凸包络提供了实用的线性时间算法，并从理论上确立了其分段有理函数的结构。

Abstract: We compute the convex envelope of (nonconvex) bivariate piecewise linear-quadratic (PLQ) functions (bivariate quadratic functions defined on a polyhedral subdivision). Our algorithm is composed of the following steps: (1) compute the convex envelope of each quadratic piece obtaining piecewise rational functions (quadratic divided by linear function) defined over a polyhedral subdivision; (2) compute the (Legendre-Fenchel) conjugate of each resulting piece to obtain piecewise quadratic functions defined over a parabolic subdivision; (3) compute the maximum of all those functions to obtain the conjugate of the original PLQ function as a piecewise quadratic function defined on a parabolic subdivision; (4) compute the conjugate of each resulting piece; and finally (5) compute the maximum over all those functions to obtain the convex envelope (biconjugate) as rational functions (quadratic divided by linear function) defined over a polyhedral subdivision. Our contribution includes a practical algorithm running in linear time, and proving that the convex envelope is a piecewise rational function.

</details>


### [82] [Where the LLM Ends and Reliable Decisions Begin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19545)
*Priyadarshan Patil,Abhishek Basu,Vikas Reddy,Abhinav Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出 ANVIL 编译器架构：仅在生成 LaTeX 数学建模时调用一次语言模型，之后由确定性编译器将 LaTeX 翻译为可执行代码，在 NLP4LP 上编译成功率 92.9%，简题准确率 98.9%、难题 91.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将自然语言优化问题描述转为求解器代码的系统在每个阶段都依赖语言模型，包括从数学建模到模型构建代码的最终翻译，这种做法存在不可靠、不可解释等隐患。作者希望将"建模"与"翻译"两件事解耦，让语言模型只用在其真正擅长的环节。

Method: ANVIL 架构：语言模型只调用一次，并在基于问题类型的约束引导下生成 LaTeX 数学建模；随后由确定性编译器完成翻译，全程不涉及语言模型。该编译器包含归一化器、递归下降解析器（生成带类型的中间表示）、将符号绑定到数据集 schema 的分析 pass，以及代码生成器。

Result: 在 NLP4LP 基准的 354 道简题与难题上，编译器为 329 道（92.9%）生成了代码，且全部走确定性路径，从未回退到模型生成代码；中位编译时间低于计时器 10ms 的分辨率。整体准确率简题 98.9%、难题 91.1%。

Conclusion: 编译成功率与求解准确率之间的差距是分析重点：作者将其分解为编译失败、返回不可行、运行时报错、目标值错误四类，并发现几乎所有错误都源于数学建模本身而非翻译过程。ANVIL 表明，只在语言模型有效之处使用它、而非当作万能工具，可在主流基准上取得优异表现。

Abstract: Systems that turn natural-language descriptions of optimization problems into solver-ready code generally use a language model at every stage, including the final translation from a mathematical formulation into executable model-building code. We propose the ANVIL compiler architecture, where we separate these concerns. A language model is called only once, assisted by constraint guidance based on problem type, to produce a LaTeX formulation. A deterministic compiler then translates that LaTeX into code with no language model involvement. We describe the deterministic compiler (a normalizer, a recursive-descent parser producing a typed intermediate representation, analysis passes that bind symbols to a dataset schema, and a code emitter) and evaluate it on the 354 easy and hard problems of the NLP4LP benchmark. The compiler produced code for 329 of 354 formulations (92.9%), taking the deterministic path in every one of those cases and never falling back to model-generated code. Median compile time was below the 10ms resolution of our timer. Overall, our formulations achieved an accuracy of 98.9% over easy problems and 91.1% for hard problems. The gap between these compilation and accuracy figures is a key point of analysis, and we analyze it: formulations that failed to compile, problems that returned as infeasible, problems raising errors at runtime, and problems returning a wrong objective. Almost all of these errors trace back to the formulation rather than to the translation. ANVIL performs exceptionally well on leading benchmarks by using language models purely where they are effective, rather than as a catch-all tool.

</details>


### [83] [PDNQP: A GPU-based Factorization-free Method for Large-scale Nonconvex Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19557)
*Zixi Chen,Haihao Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: PDNQP：一种无分解的一阶求解器，用于大规模非凸二次规划的稳定点求解，结合近端增广拉格朗日与重启加速原始-对偶混合梯度，在GPU上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解器使用的稀疏矩阵分解计算与内存开销大，且难以在GPU上高效并行，导致大规模非凸二次规划求解困难。

Method: 提出PDNQP，将近端增广拉格朗日框架与重启加速原始-对偶混合梯度结合求解强凸QP子问题。核心包括残差形式重构（避免依赖于惩罚项的法矩阵，并在外迭代间保持固定的稀疏约束算子）以及协调内层求解精度与外层进度的自适应策略。计算仅依赖稀疏矩阵-向量乘积、投影和向量运算，适合GPU执行。

Result: 在107个非凸CUTEst实例上成功率最高。容差为10^-4时，偏移几何平均运行时间加速2.6×–6.0×；容差为10^-6时聚合运行时间与最快求解器相当。在30个约百万变量的大规模合成非凸QP上，PDNQP在27个实例上满足常用外部终止准则，而其他被测求解器在同一协议下均未成功。

Conclusion: PDNQP作为无分解一阶方法，能高效求解大规模非凸二次规划并找到稳定点，在GPU友好的同时兼具高成功率与显著加速，优于现有求解器。

Abstract: Large-scale nonconvex quadratic programming remains challenging because the sparse matrix factorizations used by many existing solvers can incur substantial computational and memory costs and are difficult to parallelize efficiently on GPUs. We present PDNQP, a factorization-free first-order solver for finding stationary points of large-scale nonconvex quadratic programs. The method combines a proximal augmented-Lagrangian framework with restarted accelerated primal--dual hybrid gradient to solve the resulting strongly convex QP subproblems. Its key ingredients are a residual-form reformulation that avoids penalty-dependent normal matrices and preserves a fixed sparse constraint operator across outer iterations, and an adaptive strategy that coordinates inner-solve accuracy with outer progress. The resulting computations rely on sparse matrix--vector products, projections, and vector operations suited to GPU execution. On $107$ nonconvex CUTEst instances, PDNQP achieves the highest success rate among the tested solvers at both accuracy levels. With absolute and relative tolerances set to $10^{-4}$, it achieves speedups of $2.6\times$--$6.0\times$ in shifted geometric mean runtime; at $10^{-6}$, its aggregate runtime remains comparable to that of the fastest tested solvers. On $30$ large-scale synthetic nonconvex QPs with approximately one million variables each, PDNQP returns points satisfying the common external termination criteria on $27$ instances, while none of the other tested solvers succeeds under the same experimental protocol.

</details>


### [84] [Epidemic Control using Stackelberg Mean Field Game with Budget Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19641)
*Huaning Liu,Gokce Dayanikli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在预算约束下，将疫情防控建模为Stackelberg平均场博弈，政府作为主方通过最优控制选择社交距离与疫苗接种策略，并给出SMFE的必要条件、存在唯一性及数值求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情防控措施在现实中存在成本，当预算有限时，如何有效分配预算成为重要的公共政策问题。已有研究较少同时刻画政府的预算约束与大规模理性个体的策略互动。

Method: 构建Stackelberg平均场博弈：主方（政府）在预算支出过程约束下，为大规模理性次要个体（agent）选择社交距离指南与疫苗接种水平。政府预判个体的平均场纳什均衡（MFNE），并据此选择干预政策以最小化自身目标，从而形成双层最优控制问题。通过前向-后向常微分方程（FBODE）系统刻画Stackelberg平均场博弈均衡（SMFE）的必要条件，并给出存在性与唯一性结果；进一步提出迭代不动点算法对FBODE进行数值求解。

Result: 给出SMFE的定义及其由FBODE系统表达的必要条件，建立了均衡的存在性与唯一性结论；所提迭代不动点算法可有效数值求解FBODE，并通过计算观测展示了均衡行为特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为预算约束下的疫情防控政策设计提供了理论工具，能够刻画政府与大规模理性个体之间的层级策略互动，并为数值分析与政策评估提供可行方法。

Abstract: Epidemic mitigation comes with a price in practice, and when budget is limited, its effective allocation becomes an important question on public policy. In this paper, we study epidemic control through a Stackelberg mean-field game in which a principal, representing the government, chooses social distancing guidelines and vaccination levels for a large population of rational minor agents subject to a budget-spending process. The principal anticipates the minor agents mean-field Nash equilibrium (MFNE) and selects intervention policies to minimize her own objective, inducing a bi-level optimal control problem. We define the Stackelberg mean-field game equilibrium (SMFE) and give its necessary condition by a forward-backward ordinary differential equation (FBODE) system, and provide existence and uniqueness results. We further propose an iterative fixed-point algorithm to solve the FBODE numerically and present computational observations that illustrate the resulting equilibrium behavior.

</details>


### [85] [Optimal Strategies in a Sequential Contest with Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19706)
*Alec Pannunzio,Ashley Shaffer,Gregory Shaver,Shreyas Sundaram*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究序贯竞赛中的Stackelberg博弈：两名玩家投入努力，付出最多者胜，但努力投入存在失败概率，且玩家在失败成本与最大努力能力上存在异质性。作者依次求解追随者的最优反应策略、领导者预见追随者反应后的最优策略，并分析双方均采取最优策略时的期望效用，揭示了能力差异与失败成本对博弈结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典竞赛模型通常假设玩家能够确定性地投入所选努力水平，且玩家同质。现实中努力投入可能失败（如研发、竞选、军事冲突中的投入不确定性），且玩家在失败代价与能力上限上各不相同。本文旨在刻画这种不确定性下的序贯竞赛行为，并理解异质性如何改变均衡结果。

Method: 构建两阶段Stackelberg竞赛模型：Player 2为领导者先行选择努力，Player 1为追随者观察后行动；加入随努力递增的失败函数，刻画玩家未能成功投入所选努力水平的概率；为每位玩家设定各自的失败成本与最大努力上限（代表能力差异）。采用逆向归纳：先求追随者在给定领导者努力下的效用最大化最优策略，再在预见追随者最优反应的基础上求领导者的期望效用最大化策略，最后计算双方均最优时的期望效用并进行比较静态分析。

Result: 给出了追随者的最优策略（作为领导者努力水平的函数）；刻画了领导者的最优策略及其对追随者最优反应的预判；推导了双方均采取最优策略时的期望效用，并得出异质性能力与失败成本如何影响博弈结果的若干关键结论。

Conclusion: 在考虑努力投入失败概率的序贯竞赛中，玩家的最优策略需同时权衡努力水平、失败概率与失败成本；玩家在能力上限与失败成本上的异质性会显著改变先动优势及双方的期望效用，为理解不确定性环境下的序贯竞争提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: We study a Stackelberg model where two players compete in a sequential contest, and the player that successfully invests the most effort wins. Our formulation considers a probability that each player fails to invest their chosen level of effort, as captured by a failure function that increases with the amount of effort. The players each have their own cost of failure, as well as a maximum level of effort (capturing different abilities of the players). We prove three key results. First, we provide the optimal strategy for Player 1 (the follower), to maximize their utility given an observed effort exerted by Player 2 (the leader). Next, we characterize the optimal strategy to maximize the expected utility of Player 2, anticipating the optimal response by Player 1. Finally, we analyze the expected utilities of each player when both play their optimal strategy, and provide key insights into how heterogeneous player abilities and failure costs affect the outcomes of the game.

</details>


### [86] [(Cheap) Stochastic Policy Gradient Converges with High Probability for Linear Quadratic Regulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19712)
*Yan Li,Chengze Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究vanilla随机策略梯度方法在线性二次调节器（LQR）问题上的收敛性，证明其可在每轮仅需Õ(1)次环境交互、以polylog(1/δ)依赖达到ε-最优策略，且收敛分析不依赖LQR具体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机无模型策略优化方法在LQR上的高概率收敛此前缺乏兼具低单轮计算量与对置信水平polylog依赖的结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用vanilla随机策略梯度方法求解LQR问题，每次迭代仅需Õ(1)次环境交互以允许频繁策略改进，并确保过程稳定；收敛分析不依赖LQR的具体细节。

Result: 以概率1-δ收敛到ε-最优策略，所需交互次数为O(polylog(1/δ)/ε)，单轮计算为Õ(1)，首次实现高概率收敛与polylog置信依赖。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个对LQR兼具Õ(1)单轮计算与polylog置信依赖的随机无模型策略优化方法，其分析具有通用性，有望推广到更广泛问题类。

Abstract: We study the convergence of the vanilla stochastic policy gradient method applied to the linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem. The method is cheap in the following sense: (1) at each iteration only $\tilde{O}(1)$ interactions with the environment are needed, therefore allowing frequent policy improvement steps, and (2) to ensure stability throughout and convergence to an $ε$-optimal policy with probability $1-δ$, only $O(\mathtt{Polylog}(1/δ)/ε)$ interactions are needed. To the best of our knowledge, this appears to be the first time that a stochastic model-free policy optimization method for LQR converges with high probability with $\tilde{O}(1)$ per-iteration computation and polylogarithmic dependence on the confidence level. The convergence analysis presented here is agnostic to LQR specifics and hence could be potentially generalized to a broader class of problems.

</details>


### [87] [Extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN and stochastic optimal controls of linear system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19779)
*Hao Wu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典控制-单调性条件局限于线性框架，难以处理含非线性伴随函数、公共噪声与时滞的更一般系统；同时带有路径依赖初值控制项的随机最优控制问题存在本质困难，亟需更广的适定性理论基础。

Method: 通过引入非线性伴随函数，将控制-单调性条件由线性情形推广到非线性情形，建立extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN的适定性框架；结合精细分析工具，分别研究线性-凸问题与带时变、随机输入约束的线性-二次问题，其中关于初值的控制元素是路径依赖的。

Result: 在所建立的适定性框架下，对两类问题均证明了最优控制的存在性与唯一性，并给出了显式的闭式表示。

Conclusion: 非线性控制-单调性条件的推广为含公共噪声、时滞与路径依赖初值的随机最优控制提供了统一且有效的分析框架，所得显式解具有理论与应用价值。

Abstract: The research studies the extended mean-field anticipated forward-backward stochastic delayed differential equations with common noise (extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN) by expanding the domination-monotonicity conditions. We generalize the conditions from a linear setting to a nonlinear one by including nonlinear adjoint functions which are key to guaranteeing the well-posedness of extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN. Utilizing this broader well-posedness framework for extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN, combined with other refined analytical tools, we examine two classes of stochastic optimal control problems. These include a linear-convex problem and a linear-quadratic problem with input constraints that can be both time-dependent and random. Moreover, the control elements regarding the initial values are path-dependent, which will cause some essential difficulties. For each problem, we establish the existence and uniqueness of optimal controls and provide their explicit, closed-form representations.

</details>


### [88] [Pairwise comparisons meet optimal transport theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19841)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较理论中的一致性、优先级推导与不一致性评估长期作为相对独立的问题被研究，缺乏统一的理论框架。作者希望借助最优传输这一成熟工具，为这些经典问题提供共同的形式化基础，并由此获得新的方法学启示。

Method: 以最优传输为理论透镜，将成对比较矩阵与双随机缩放、熵正则化传输问题相联系；利用Sinkhorn缩放算法作为核心工具，把一致性、优先级导出与不一致性度量纳入统一的最优传输框架，并在此基础上构造新的刻画与指标。

Result: 作为该关联的方法学产物，本文得到三项具体成果：一致性的新刻画、一种基于Sinkhorn的优先级计算方法，以及一个基于熵的不一致性指标；并对这些方案进行了理论分析与数值实验，与文献中的既有方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 成对比较理论可在最优传输框架下被统一处理，Sinkhorn缩放为一致性分析、优先级推导和不一致性评估提供了共同语言；所提方法在理论和数值上均具有可行性，并与经典方法形成有意义的对照。

Abstract: In this paper, we reinterpret pairwise comparison theory through the lens of optimal transport. The main contribution is the connection established between pairwise comparison matrices, doubly stochastic scaling, and entropy-regularized transport problems. Using Sinkhorn scaling, we show how consistency, priority derivation, and inconsistency assessment can be studied within a common optimal-transport framework. As methodological consequences of this connection, we obtain a new characterization of consistency, a Sinkhorn-based prioritization method, and an entropy-based inconsistency index. We study these proposals both analytically and numerically and compare them with established methods from the literature

</details>


### [89] [Percolation Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19905)
*Melissa González García,Guillaume Vigeral*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究渗流马尔可夫决策过程（PMDPs），证明均匀值存在性与0-最优策略，并在伯努利收益下给出均匀值的连续性结果，同时通过一维例子揭示最优策略的复杂性，最后提出降维逼近方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者在d维整数格上推动令牌，边收益随机且在决策前揭示，目标是最大化固定视界内的平均累积收益。该模型将渗流理论与马尔可夫决策过程结合，具有理论与应用价值，但均匀值的存在性、最优策略性质及与渗流临界阈值的关系尚不明确。

Method: 构建PMDP模型：令牌在d维整数格上按确定性转移移动，边收益随机并预先揭示。分析无限视界下的均匀值与0-最优策略；针对伯努利收益情形研究均匀值的连续性；构造一维示例说明最优策略的复杂性；提出维度提升（降维逼近）性质。

Result: 证明了均匀值（视界趋于无穷时）的存在性及0-最优策略的存在性；在伯努利收益下建立了均匀值的连续性结果；一维例子表明最优策略可能非常复杂；维度提升性质使PMDPs可由低维PMDPs逼近。

Conclusion: PMDPs具有良好的理论性质（均匀值与0-最优策略存在），但其最优策略可能高度复杂。降维逼近性质为利用低维模型近似渗流理论中的临界概率阈值提供了潜在工具。

Abstract: We study Percolation Markov Decision Processes (PMDPs), in which the decision maker repeatedly moves a token through the d-dimensional integer lattice with deterministic transitions and random payoffs assigned to the edges. Payoffs are revealed before the beginning of the decision process and the decision maker aims to maximize the average accumulated payoff over a fixed horizon. We establish the existence of the uniform value (as the horizon tends to infinity) and 0-optimal strategies. In the particular case of Bernoulli payoffs, we establish continuity results for the uniform value. We also present several one-dimensional examples to illustrate that optimal strategies may be very complex. Then, we introduce a dimensional lifting property that allows PMDPs to be approximated by PMDPs of lower dimension and could be used to approximate critical probability thresholds in percolation theory.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统日前拍卖型电力市场模型通常只考虑经济性调度，未将排放（污染）作为可测量、可约束的变量纳入系统运营商的最优决策；本文旨在构建一个能刻画监管者限制排放的电力市场模型，以弥补既有文献在环境约束建模上的不足。

Method: 构建受监管的日前拍卖型电力市场模型，系统运营商在最优调度中可测量并限制发电商的排放；从理论上证明该模型对既有电力市场模型的推广性质；采用标准灵敏度分析工具度量污染变量对市场均衡的影响，并给出数值算例验证。

Result: 理论证明该市场模型是此前若干电力市场模型的推广；灵敏度分析量化了排放相关变量对市场均衡的影响；数值算例展示了该影响效果。

Conclusion: 该模型为含排放约束的日前电力市场提供了统一分析框架，既可退化为已有模型，又能通过灵敏度分析刻画污染变量与市场均衡之间的关系，为监管者设定排放限制提供量化依据。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for a regulated day-ahead type auction-based electrical market in which the system operator can measure and limit the producer's emissions when choosing its optimal dispatch. We prove properties of this market model that describe it as a generalization to other previous works in the electrical market literature. Furthermore, we use standard sensitivity analysis tools to measure the impact of these pollution variables on market equilibria and present numerical examples of this effect.

</details>


### [91] [Distributed Continuous-Time Optimization on the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3) over Tree Interaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20031)
*Dongming Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究无向树图上三维旋转群的连续时间分布式优化，提出固定增益非光滑协议，结合黎曼梯度下降与基于符号函数的共识反馈，在边界条件下证明有限时间精确共识并给出收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三维旋转群上做分布式优化时，测地强凸性本身不足以保证操作球的不变性（论文给出反例），因此需要新的协议设计与边界条件来确保解的适定性并实现共识。

Method: 针对无向树图、异构局部代价，提出固定增益非光滑协议：局部黎曼梯度下降 + 由相邻相对旋转的Lie群对数构造的符号型共识反馈；施加内指边界条件；构造非光滑内在不一致性Lyapunov函数，利用树结构的簇收缩论证导出均匀耗散界。

Result: 在有界局部梯度下给出显式增益比条件，保证每个Filippov解实现精确有限时间共识，并得到仅依赖智能体数量的收敛时间估计；共识后滑动动力学等价于总目标的缩放黎曼梯度流；当极小值点位于球内部时，按测地距离指数收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为旋转群上的分布式优化提供了带有限时间共识保证的非光滑协议设计框架，明确了边界不变性、增益条件与收敛速率之间的关系。

Abstract: We study continuous-time distributed optimization on the three-dimensional rotation group over undirected tree graphs, where agents with heterogeneous local costs seek a common attitude minimizing the geodesically strongly convex sum of their costs on a geodesically convex operating ball. We propose a nonsmooth protocol with fixed gains that combines local Riemannian gradient descent with signum-based consensus feedback computed from the Lie-group logarithms of relative rotations between neighboring agents. Since geodesic strong convexity alone does not guarantee invariance, as demonstrated by a certified counterexample, we impose an inward-pointing boundary condition and prove that every Filippov solution with all agents initialized in the ball remains there. We construct a nonsmooth intrinsic disagreement Lyapunov function and derive a uniform dissipation bound through a cluster-contraction argument exploiting the tree structure. With bounded local gradients, this bound gives an explicit gain-ratio condition under which we establish exact finite-time consensus for every such solution and obtain a settling-time estimate requiring no structural graph constant beyond the number of agents. After consensus, we identify the sliding dynamics as a scaled Riemannian gradient flow of the summed objective. When the minimizer lies in the interior of the ball, the dynamics converge to it exponentially in geodesic distance. Simulations illustrate our theoretical results.

</details>

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of designing distributed optimization algorithms. Different to existing methods that usually give sufficient conditions and conservative convergence rates, we propose a framework for determining the exact worst-case convergence rate. The worst-case convergence is defined over the set of $μ$-strongly convex and $L$-Lipschitz objective functions and the set of connected graphs with given algebraic connectivity. Focusing on the Distributed Inexact Gradient Tracking (DIGing)--an algorithm widely deployed in applications and extensively studied in recent years, we develop a systematic design methodology by integrating techniques from graph signal processing and robust control theory. First, we present a novel decomposition structure that enables partial subsystem decoupling. Then we derive explicit formulas for the exact worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. Numerical experiments validate the correctness and effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 针对固定参考熵正则Wasserstein重心问题：先在优化前选定重心空间上的概率测度η，使第i个传输计划相对于ν_i⊗η正则化；在紧度量空间且成本连续的前提下，通过原–对偶分析与指数原–对偶关系证明对偶最大化位势的存在性，并推导重心的显式密度。

Result: 对偶问题在连续函数类中存在最大化位势；最优传输计划元组与重心存在且唯一；最大化位势通过指数原–对偶关系恢复最优计划，并给出重心关于η的显式密度，该密度连续且严格为正；位势从成本继承正则性，且在自然的加性规范变换下唯一。

Conclusion: 结果强化了已知的“值对偶”理论，为固定参考正则重心问题提供了完整的连续原–对偶刻画。

Abstract: We study the fixed-reference entropy-regularized Wasserstein barycenter problem, in which a probability measure $η$ on the barycenter space is chosen before optimization and the $i$th transport plan is regularized relative to $ν_i\otimesη$. Earlier work established a dual formulation for this model and equality between the primal and dual optimal values. However, equality of values does not by itself guarantee that the dual supremum is achieved. Our main result shows that, on compact metric spaces with continuous transport costs, the dual problem admits maximizing potentials in the original class of continuous functions. For completeness, we also establish existence and uniqueness of the optimal tuple of transport plans and of the barycenter. The maximizing potentials recover the optimal plans through exponential primal--dual relations and give an explicit density of the barycenter with respect to $η$. This density is continuous and strictly positive. We further show that the potentials inherit regularity from the costs and are unique up to the natural additive gauge transformations. These results strengthen the known value-duality theory and provide a complete continuous primal--dual description of the fixed-reference regularized barycenter problem.

</details>


### [94] [Near-Optimal Pure Single-Loop Extragradient Method for Strongly Convex--Strongly Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20327)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极小极大优化方法常需内层求解、精度调度或分阶段重启，结构复杂且实现成本高；本文旨在设计一种固定参数、无需上述机制的简洁单环算法，同时达到最优的条件数依赖阶。

Method: 在确定性无约束设定下，针对一般非线性耦合的光滑强凸-强凹极小极大问题，提出纯单环阻尼外推梯度法。算法使用固定参数，在初始一次查询后每轮进行两次全梯度评估，并引入辅助反馈递推，无需内层求解、精度调度或分阶段重启。

Result: 建立了最后一次迭代的线性收敛，并证明将到鞍点的平方欧氏距离降到初始值的 ε 比例所需全梯度查询为 O(√(κ_xκ_y) log(2κ_xκ_y/ε))，其中 κ_x=L/μ_x，κ_y=L/μ_y。该复杂度在对数因子内达到最优条件数阶。数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过固定的显式更新，所提单环阻尼外推梯度法在无需复杂机制的情况下实现了最优条件数阶的收敛保证，兼具理论最优性与实现简洁性。

Abstract: We study smooth strongly convex--strongly concave minimax optimization with general nonlinear coupling in the deterministic unconstrained setting. We propose a pure single-loop damped extragradient method with fixed parameters and two new full-gradient evaluations per iteration after one initialization query. The method uses an auxiliary feedback recursion and requires no inner solves, accuracy schedules, or staged restarts. We establish last-iterate linear convergence and show that reducing the squared Euclidean distance to the saddle point to an $\varepsilon$ fraction of its initial value requires $O(\sqrt{κ_xκ_y}\log(2κ_xκ_y/\varepsilon))$ full-gradient queries, where $κ_x=L/μ_x$ and $κ_y=L/μ_y$. This bound attains the optimal condition-number order up to logarithmic factors through fixed explicit updates. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [95] [Constraint Qualifications and Gradient Flows for Block Vanishing Constraint Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20368)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 数值实验在若干桁架拓扑设计实例上开展，包含两个甚至三个施加载荷，结果表明该方法具有鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 块消失约束问题的约束资格理论可自经典MPVC自然扩展，且所提出的分片梯度流方法在较弱的GCQ条件下即可保证收敛并直接获得强稳定点，适用于多载荷桁架拓扑设计。

Abstract: Mathematical Programs with Blocks of Vanishing Constraints (MP-BVCs) are a challenging class of optimization problems due to the loss of standard constraint qualifications. Based on existing theory for classical MPVCs, i.e., MPBVC with block size one, we extend the constraint qualification theory for MPBVCs and show that the known LICQ-type condition for MPBVCs implies the Guignard Constraint Qualification (GCQ). A novel piecewise gradient flow approach converges under GCQ. It directly computes strongly stationary points and is an extension of the piecewise gradient flow approach for classic MPVC problems. Numerical experiments on several truss topology design instances with two and even three applied load forces demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [96] [Geometry and Convergence of Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20400)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究小正则化情形下二次正则化最优传输的尖锐收敛速率，刻画正则化最优解支撑集的稀疏性、几何结构及对偶势的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化二次正则化最优传输在小正则化 $\varepsilon$ 情形下的收敛速率与解的稀疏性，并识别收敛失效的正则性阈值。

Method: 针对二次代价的二次正则化最优传输，在光滑边缘密度情形下分析正则化最优解支撑集与 Brenier 图的距离及其截面几何，并结合对偶势的 Hessian 双侧一致界进行估计。

Result: 在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 光滑边缘密度下，支撑集位于距 Brenier 图 $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$ 量级范围内，其每个截面被该量级半径的球夹逼；对偶势满足一致双侧 Hessian 界，并以 $\varepsilon^{2/(d+2)}$ 速率一致收敛，其梯度以 $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$ 速率收敛。所得速率是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 建立了二次正则化最优传输的尖锐收敛速率，揭示了支撑集几何与对偶势之间的密切联系，并确定了收敛性失效的正则性阈值。

Abstract: We establish sharp convergence rates for quadratically regularized optimal transport with quadratic cost in the regime of small regularization $\varepsilon$. In particular, we quantify the sparsity of the support of the regularized optimizer. For smooth marginal densities in $\mathbb{R}^d$, this support lies within a distance of order $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$ from the Brenier graph, and every section of the support is sandwiched between balls with radii of that order. The geometry of the support is closely linked to the dual potentials. We show that the potentials satisfy uniform two-sided Hessian bounds and converge uniformly at rate $\varepsilon^{2/(d+2)}$, while their gradients converge at rate $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$. The rates are sharp, and we further identify the regularity threshold where convergence breaks down.

</details>


### [97] [Convergence rate of the moment-SOS hierarchy for univariate polynomial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20544)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩-SOS层级是求解多项式优化的常用方法，但其收敛速率（尤其是一元情形下边界退化对速率的影响）尚缺精确刻画，作者希望确定一元POP松弛误差的收敛指数并判定其最优性。

Method: 证明结合了有限生成一元二次模的结构性质与一个Chebyshev多项式构造：该构造在控制证书次数的同时近似恢复可行集的自然生成元；此外构造了一个四次的一元例子，并给出与之等价的立方一元问题及在极小点处含尖点奇异的双变量POP表述。

Result: 对每个固定的一元POP，松弛误差被O(1/r²)界定，其中r为松弛阶数；可行集多项式描述中的边界退化只影响常数而不改变收敛指数。所给四次例子的松弛误差恰为1/(2r(r-1))。

Conclusion: 一元POP的矩-SOS松弛具有二次收敛速率且该速率最优；边界退化不降低收敛指数，仅影响常数，且该现象可联系到立方一元问题与含尖点奇异的双变量POP。

Abstract: We study the convergence rate of the moment-SOS (sum-of-squares) hierarchy for polynomial optimization problems (POPs) on a bounded subset of the real line described by arbitrary polynomial inequalities. We prove that, for every fixed univariate POP, the relaxation error is bounded by $O(1/r^2)$, where $r$ is the relaxation order. In particular, boundary degeneracies in the polynomial description of the feasible set affect the constant but not the convergence exponent. The proof combines the structure of finitely generated univariate quadratic modules with a Chebyshev polynomial construction that approximately recovers the natural generators of the feasible set while controlling the degree of the certificate. We also give an elementary degree-four example for which the relaxation error is exactly $1/(2r(r-1))$, showing that the quadratic rate is optimal. Equivalent reformulations connect this example to a cubic univariate problem and to a bivariate POP whose feasible set has a cusp singularity at the minimizer.

</details>


### [98] [Complexity Of Output Feedback Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20636)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Abraar Chaudhry,Ijay Narang,Yukai Tang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输出反馈镇定是控制理论中最著名的开放问题之一，其计算复杂性此前尚不明确。作者希望厘清该问题在多项式时间可解性上的界限。

Method: 通过计算复杂性归约（在 P ≠ NP 假设下）证明：不存在多项式时间或伪多项式时间算法，能为带线性控制器的线性动力系统构造输出反馈镇定控制器；分别处理连续时间与离散时间情形，并构造一类可镇定系统族来证明其镇定控制器无法在多项式时间内以标准形式给出。

Result: 证明了输出反馈镇定问题的多项式时间（及伪多项式时间）不可解性，即该问题在 P ≠ NP 下是计算困难的；同时给出了一类可镇定但无法多项式时间写出标准镇定控制器的线性系统族。

Conclusion: 输出反馈镇定的计算复杂性被确定为难解，解决了控制理论中一个长期开放的问题，表明设计镇定控制器的困难本质上源于计算复杂性而非仅工程实现。

Abstract: We show that unless P = NP, there cannot be a polynomial-time (or even pseudo-polynomial-time) algorithm for output feedback stabilization of a linear dynamical system with a linear controller. This settles one of the best-known open problems in control theory. The result holds in both continuous and discrete time. We also present a family of stabilizable linear dynamical systems for which no polynomial-time algorithm can write down a stabilizing controller in its standard representation.

</details>


### [99] [The First-Order Oracle Complexity of Lipschitz Convex Optimization in Nondual Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20687)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Brian Bullins,Cristóbal Guzmán,Mathieu Molina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\ell_p$ 球上一阶黑箱凸优化，在 $\ell_q$ 范数 Lipschitz 目标下证明确实可利用更小可行集几何加速收敛，达到 $\widetilde O(1/T)$ 等速率，并匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 COLT 开放问题（Guz15b）的非光滑版本：当可行集为更小的 $\ell_p$ 球（$p<q$）、目标关于 $\ell_q$ 范数 Lipschitz 时，凸优化收敛率能否优于一般假设下的经典 $O(1/\sqrt{T})$。同时推进 Sridharan 提出的更一般问题。

Method: 引入新的在线学习博弈：比较器用截至目前观察到的仿射损失的最大值评估。用组合在线学习量——顺序 fat-shattering 维数——给出该博弈值的上下界，并刻画 $\ell_p/\ell_q$ 情形。方法适用于可行集 $X$ 与可能次梯度集 $H$ 均为凸、中心对称且满足某类 minmax 定理的情形。

Result: 对 $\ell_1$ 球上欧氏 Lipschitz 凸优化得到 $\widetilde O(1/T)$，改进经典 $O(1/\sqrt{T})$；一般情形匹配已有下界至对数因子。几何副产品：在多种 Banach 几何中估计样本凸包到其均值的期望距离，给出 Wendel 定理的定量版本，适用于有界一般分布而非仅中心对称分布。

Conclusion: 更小可行集的几何可显著加速凸优化；顺序 fat-shattering 维数是核心分析工具；结果推广到一类凸、中心对称可行集与次梯度集，并为凸包-均值距离提供新的定量估计。

Abstract: We study first-order black-box convex optimization over an $\ell_p$-ball for objectives Lipschitz in the $\ell_q$-norm, solving in the affirmative the nonsmooth version of the COLT open question (Guz15b) on whether the geometry of a smaller feasible set ($p < q$) can improve convergence rates in convex optimization, and matching prior lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. Our rates include \(\widetilde O(1/T)\) for convex Euclidean-Lipschitz optimization over the $\ell_1$-ball, improving on the $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ classical rate under general assumptions. The key technical device is a new online learning game, where the comparator is evaluated using the maximum of affine losses observed so far. We bound the value of this game above and below in terms of a combinatorial online learning quantity: the sequential fat-shattering dimension, which we characterize for the $\ell_p / \ell_q$ case. Our results generally apply when the feasible set $X$ and the set of possible subgradients $H$ are convex, centrally symmetric, and admit a type of minmax theorem, advancing on a fundamental question by Sridharan [Sri12, Section 10.1.2, Q3]. As a geometric consequence of our analysis, of independent interest, we obtain estimates for the expected distance of a convex hull of samples to their mean in several Banach geometries, a version of the celebrated Wendel's theorem (Wen62), but quantitative and for bounded general distributions as opposed to centrally symmetric ones.

</details>


### [100] [Stable Movement for Nondual Lipschitz Convex Optimization: Efficiency and Nearly Optimal Oracle Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20701)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Cristóbal Guzmán*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究 G-Lipschitz 凸函数在 ℓp 球上的 ℓq 范数优化的一阶 oracle 复杂度，针对 p<q 情形给出误差界 Õ(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)+})，高效实现近最优速率，解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题的非光滑端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 COLT 2015 开放问题（Guz15b）的非光滑端：在 ℓp 球约束、ℓq 范数 Lipschitz 的一般设定下（尤其 p<q 的非光滑情形），高效实现近最优的一阶 oracle 复杂度。

Method: 将凸 Lipschitz 优化归约为在演化 bundle 的次水平集上追踪嵌套凸集问题：每次查询要么找到低函数值点，要么在当前 bundle 次水平集中产生深割并追踪之。通过选择子的稳定性与深割强制移动之间的二分法界定迭代次数。对 R B_p^d 的嵌套子集引入"稳定中心"概念，其 ℓq 范数移动被界为 Õ(RT^{1-1/p+(1/q-1/2)+})，并用 Monte Carlo 平均的选择子在实算术模型下多项式时间实现。

Result: p<q 时得到误差 Õ_{p,q}(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)+})；例如 p=1,q=2（ℓ1 球上欧氏 Lipschitz）时速率达 Õ(GR/T)。稳定中心的移动界在高维下近最优，Monte Carlo 平均选择子以高概率达到近最优速率。

Conclusion: 该工作高效实现了近最优收敛速率，解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题的非光滑端，并引入稳定中心这一新概念，为嵌套凸集追踪与凸 Lipschitz 优化建立了统一框架。

Abstract: We study efficient algorithms for realizing the first-order oracle complexity of optimization of $G$-Lipschitz convex functions with respect to the $\ell_{q}$-norm over an $\ell_{p}$-ball of radius $R$, where $1\leq p,q\leq \infty$. For $p<q$, we obtain error $\widetilde{O}_{p,q}(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)_{+}})$ after $T$ oracle queries, efficiently realizing the nearly optimal rates of (MBG+26), thereby resolving the nonsmooth end of the COLT 2015 open problem (Guz15b). In particular, the rate is $\widetilde{O}(GR/T)$ for Euclidean Lipschitzness over an $\ell_1$-ball of radius $R$ ($p=1,q=2$). Our solution consists of reducing convex Lipschitz optimization to the chasing nested convex sets problem in sublevel sets of an evolving bundle (LNN95; BBE+20): at each query we either find a point with low function value or we produce a deep cut in the current sublevel of the bundle, that we chase. The dichotomy between stability of selectors and forced movement by deep cuts bounds the number of iterations of the algorithm near optimally. For nested subsets of $R B_{p}^{d}$, we introduce a novel notion of stable center whose movement is bounded by $\widetilde{O}_{p,q}(RT^{1-1/p+(1/q-1/2)_{+}})$ in the $\ell_{q}$-norm after $T$ steps, which we show is nearly optimal in high dimensions. A Monte Carlo average of the proposed selector achieves near-optimal rates with high probability and can be implemented in polynomial time for our optimization algorithm in the real-arithmetic model.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [AthenaZero: A low-inertia, bimanual robot for dynamic manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19194)
*Andrew S. Morgan,Gregory Xie,Capprin Bass,Rachel Thomasson,Chunpeng Wang,Erfan Shahriari,Harrison Busa,Oluwaseun Araromi,Joseph Aronov,Michael Burgess,Velin D. Dimitrov,Matthew A. Estrada,Faris Hamdi,Samuel Kendig,Taeyoon Lee,Jose Oscar Mur-Miranda,Emma Sommers,Paul Titchener,Margaret Wang,Achu Wilson,Mark Yeatman,Osman Dogan Yirmibesoglu,Alfred A. Rizzi,Annan Mozeika,Nicolas Rojas,Lael Odhner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AthenaZero是一款双臂机械臂，通过准直驱驱动与传动远程化设计降低惯量，实现约与人类相当（比传统机械臂低约一个数量级）的末端等效质量，适用于高速动态操作，并完成投掷、接球、击球三类棒球式任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械臂惯量大、力矩透明性差，难以在毫秒级时间尺度上完成高加速度的动态操作与复杂人机/机器人间交互。

Method: 采用准直驱（quasi-direct drive）驱动与传动远程化（transmission remotization）技术，在不牺牲控制权限的前提下最小化系统惯量，并据此给出设计方法。

Result: 投掷速度超过30 m/s；在7.3 m短距离内接球与击球速度超过14 m/s；进一步实现机器人对机器人、人对机器人的击球练习与接球游戏。

Conclusion: 该设计兼具低惯量与固有力矩透明性，证明AthenaZero在高加速度、人类可比时间尺度任务中具有有效性与适应性。

Abstract: AthenaZero is a bimanual manipulator designed to minimize inertia without compromising control authority. By utilizing quasi-direct drive actuation and transmission remotization techniques, the system achieves an effective endpoint mass comparable to that of a human---about an order of magnitude less than conventional robot manipulators. This characteristic, combined with its inherent torque transparency, makes AthenaZero exceptionally well-suited for dynamic manipulation. We describe the methodology} that led to this design and demonstrate the robot's capabilities on three baseball-inspired tasks: throwing, catching, and batting, which showcase complex interactions on human-comparable timescales where milliseconds matter. AthenaZero was capable of throwing at speeds in excess of 30 m/s, with catching and batting at speeds in excess of 14 m/s over a short 7.3 m distance. Batting practice and a game of catch were subsequently performed in robot-to-robot and human-to-robot variations, showcasing the efficacy and adaptability of our system in tasks that require high acceleration.

</details>


### [102] [DITTO: Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19196)
*Joaquin Palacios,Katelyn Lee,Cheng Zhang,Zhanpeng He,Matei Ciocarlie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DITTO 是一个用于透明遥操作的灵巧接口，通过机械臂手与运动学等价的动力外骨骼的协同设计，统一了野外数据采集与带关节级力反馈的双向遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度灵巧手操作数据的采集面临一个两难：遥操作保证部署一致性但缺乏力反馈；手持（野外）系统提供自然的力透明性，却在部署时存在视觉具身差距。同时，接口需同时捕捉丰富的手部运动并渲染精确操作所必需的接触交互。

Method: 提出 DITTO（Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation），基于解剖学信息，协同设计一只 7 自由度灵巧机械手与一个运动学等价的电动外骨骼。外骨骼与机械手之间采用 1:1 执行器映射，使手持（野外）数据采集与带关节级力反馈的双向遥操作在单一平台上统一。

Result: DITTO 外骨骼覆盖了操作者食指到拇指的自然工作空间；并通过在接触密集任务上学习策略，展示了 DITTO 的灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: DITTO 通过机械手与外骨骼的解剖学协同设计及 1:1 执行器映射，弥合了遥操作与手持式数据采集之间的权衡，实现了兼具力反馈与部署一致性的灵巧操作数据采集与遥操作平台。

Abstract: Collecting data for manipulation with high-DOF hands is challenging, as interfaces must capture rich hand motion while rendering the contact interactions essential for precise manipulation. Existing data collection approaches face a trade-off: teleoperation ensures deployment consistency but lacks force feedback, while handheld (in-the-wild) systems provide natural force transparency but introduce a visual embodiment gap at deployment. We present DITTO, a Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation, which resolves this through the anatomically informed co-design of a dexterous 7-DOF robotic hand and a kinematically equivalent motorized exoskeleton. A 1-to-1 actuator mapping between the exoskeleton and robotic hand enables handheld (in-the-wild) data collection and bilateral teleoperation with joint-level force feedback unified in a single platform. We demonstrate that the DITTO exoskeleton spans the operator's natural index-to-thumb workspace, and showcase DITTO's dexterous capabilities via learned policies on contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [103] [ULOHA: An Underwater Bimanual Robot System for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19200)
*Masato Kobayashi,Takeru Tsunoori*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ULОHA 是一个水下双臂机器人学习平台，结合定制主从硬件与 LeRobot 软件扩展，支持遥操作、多视角感知、示教采集、策略训练与自主部署，并在真实水下环境中评估了 ACT、Diffusion Policy 与 SmolVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下视觉运动策略学习多集中于单臂，双臂模仿学习主要在空气中研究；水下环境存在气泡扰动、浮力驱动运动以及实时动作分块等耦合的感知与物理效应，缺乏统一的双臂水下学习实验平台。

Method: 构建 ULOHA 平台：定制主从（leader–follower）硬件，并在 LeRobot 上做软件扩展，整合遥操作、多视角传感、示教采集、策略训练与自主部署。开展真实机器人实验，覆盖臂间迁移、共享物体操作和浮力驱动拦截等协调双臂行为；对比 ACT、Diffusion Policy 与视觉-语言-动作模型 SmolVLA，考察气泡扰动、浮力驱动物体运动、动作执行时域与实时分块；另设单臂研究，检验空气与水中之间的策略迁移。

Result: 平台成功实现多种协调水下双臂行为，包括臂间迁移、共享物体操作与浮力驱动拦截；在水下条件下对 ACT、Diffusion Policy、SmolVLA 进行了评估，并分析了气泡扰动、浮力驱动运动、动作执行时域和实时分块的影响；单臂研究表明，在所测条件下，跨越空气与水的示教可支持两种介质中的执行。

Conclusion: ULОHA 为研究水下环境中耦合感知与物理效应下的双臂机器人学习提供了统一实验平台，并揭示了空中学习方法与执行策略在水下的适用性，以及跨介质策略迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Underwater visuomotor policy learning has focused primarily on single manipulators, while bimanual imitation learning has been studied largely in air. We present ULOHA, an underwater bimanual robot learning platform that combines custom-designed leader--follower hardware with software extensions to LeRobot, integrating teleoperation, multi-view sensing, demonstration collection, policy training, and autonomous deployment. Real-robot experiments demonstrate a range of coordinated underwater bimanual behaviors, including inter-arm transfer, shared-object manipulation, and buoyancy-driven interception. We evaluate ACT, Diffusion Policy, and the vision--language--action model SmolVLA on the platform. We investigate how learning methods and execution strategies developed for manipulation in air perform underwater, examining bubble disturbances, buoyancy-driven object motion, action-execution horizons, and real-time chunking. A separate single-arm study examines policy transfer between air and water and shows that demonstrations spanning both media support execution in both under the tested conditions. ULOHA provides a unified experimental platform for studying underwater bimanual robot learning under the coupled perceptual and physical effects of underwater environments. Additional material: https://mertcookimg.github.io/uloha/

</details>


### [104] [REACT: A Fully Spiking State-Space Model for Real-Time Event-Driven Temporal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19204)
*Geoffroy Keime,Nicolas Cuperlier,Benoit R. Cottereau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出REACT——一种全脉冲状态空间模型，直接逐个处理原始事件流，无需时间累积，实现低延迟的事件驱动时间感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的机器人视觉感知需随传感流连续演化，但多数基于学习的事件相机方法将事件累积为帧或时间箱，引入积分延迟，限制快速反应能力。

Method: REACT采用复值脉冲神经元C-SiLIF，其连续时间动力学由物理事件间隔驱动，内部状态以单个事件的时间分辨率演化；在无目标边界框或定位输入条件下，对全视场事件流进行手势识别与碰撞时间（TTC）估计。

Result: 在EvTTC上TTC相对误差9.59%，端到端推理延迟4.6 ms，与最优学习方法相差0.15个百分点且无需目标先验；该延迟对应车辆仅4 cm位移（最快对比方法为1 m）。支持随时TTC预测、零样本迁移至不同驾驶序列及INT8量化，每32,768个事件能耗由18.5 mJ降至2.8 mJ。

Conclusion: 事件驱动的脉冲状态空间动力学可为反应式机器人系统提供低延迟、持续更新的时间感知。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in dynamic environments require visual perception that evolves continuously with the incoming sensory stream. Event cameras provide microsecond temporal resolution and asynchronous sensing, but most learning-based methods accumulate events into frames or temporal bins, introducing an integration delay that can limit fast reaction. Here we propose REACT, a fully spiking state-space model for event-driven temporal perception that processes raw events one by one, without temporal accumulation. REACT uses a complex-valued spiking neuron, C-SiLIF, whose continuous-time dynamics are driven by the physical inter-event interval, allowing its internal state to evolve at the temporal resolution of individual events. We evaluate REACT on gesture recognition and time-to-collision (TTC) estimation from full-field event streams, without a target bounding box or localization input. On EvTTC, REACT achieves a 9.59% relative TTC error with 4.6 ms end-to-end inference latency, within 0.15 percentage points of the best learned method while requiring no target prior. At the dataset's mean approach speed, this latency corresponds to only 4 cm of vehicle motion, compared with 1 m for the fastest competing learned method. REACT further supports anytime TTC prediction, zero-shot transfer to a different driving sequence, and INT8 quantization, reducing the estimated energy consumption from 18.5 to 2.8 mJ per 32,768 events. These results show that event-driven spiking state-space dynamics can provide low-latency, continuously updated temporal perception for reactive robotic systems.

</details>


### [105] [4D Radar Perception Algorithms for Autonomous Driving: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19216)
*Xumin Wu,Jun Zhou,Jilin Mei,Chen Min,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇4D毫米波雷达感知算法综述，按感知任务与算法演进组织，涵盖雷达基础、目标级感知、运动与定位、局部与稠密空间感知及动态场景理解，并比较纯雷达学习、多模态融合与跨模态监督/知识蒸馏三类范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D毫米波雷达感知算法近年来快速发展，研究范围已从信号处理和目标检测扩展到语义分割、运动估计、占据预测与动态场景重建，领域内缺乏以任务演进为主线的系统梳理。

Method: 采用任务导向的综述方法：先介绍雷达基本原理、数据表示与质量增强方法；随后按目标级感知、运动与定位、局部与稠密空间感知、动态场景理解逐层评述；横向比较纯雷达学习、多模态融合、跨模态监督与知识蒸馏；重点分析俯仰、多普勒测量及雷达物理先验在各任务中的利用方式；并总结现有数据集的任务覆盖、输入数据、标注与评测协议。

Result: 系统梳理了各感知任务与算法的发展脉络，明确了不同研究范式的适用性与差异，并厘清了现有数据集对各类研究方向的经验支撑情况。

Conclusion: 总结了4D雷达自动驾驶感知的共性挑战与未来方向，提出从稀疏目标感知走向动态空间理解的任务导向视角。

Abstract: Research on 4D millimeter-wave radar perception algorithms has flourished in recent years, extending from signal processing and object detection to semantic segmentation, motion estimation, occupancy prediction, and dynamic scene reconstruction. This review organizes the field according to the evolution of perception tasks and algorithms. It first introduces radar fundamentals, data representations, and quality-enhancement methods, and then reviews object-level perception, motion and localization, local and dense spatial perception, and dynamic scene understanding. Across these directions, we compare radar-only learning, multimodal fusion, and cross-modal supervision and knowledge distillation. Particular attention is paid to how elevation, Doppler measurements, and radar physical priors are exploited across tasks. We further summarize the task coverage, input data, annotations, and evaluation protocols of existing datasets, clarifying the empirical support for different research directions. Finally, we discuss the common challenges and future directions of 4D radar perception for autonomous driving. This review provides a task-oriented perspective on the transition from sparse object perception to dynamic spatial understanding.

</details>


### [106] [Grasping by interconnection: robust closing motions from coarse object templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19228)
*Julien Vanderheyden,Guillaume Drion,Fulvio Forni,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于虚拟模型控制的抓取规划器，利用粗略模板、人类抓取类型、物体中心交互和柔顺滑动接触，使闭合动作本身具备对物体尺寸与位姿误差的容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手常需抓取形状、尺寸和位姿仅近似已知的物体；现有规划器多依赖精确模型或用反馈纠错，但闭合动作自身能容忍多大误差尚不明确。

Method: 设计基于四条原则的运动规划器：物体粗略模板、人类抓取类型、以物体为中心的交互、用柔顺滑动接触代替预设接触点；通过虚拟模型控制实现，并在Shadow灵巧手上评估。

Result: 无反馈条件下，规划闭合动作可容忍约1cm的尺寸误差及数厘米、数十度的位姿误差，在27种测试条件中的25种优于先进数据驱动规划器；成功抓取80件日常物品中的82.5%，并在自主流程中成功。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性可被设计进闭合动作本身，而非仅依赖反馈；该规划器为不确定环境下的可靠操作提供路径，未来将结合物理手上的自适应反馈控制。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands must often grasp objects whose shape, size, and pose are known only approximately. Grasp planners typically require accurate object models or correct errors with feedback, but how much inaccuracy a closing motion can tolerate on its own remains unclear. To address this question, we designed a motion planner based on four principles: a coarse template of the object, human grasp types, an object-centric interaction, and compliant, sliding contacts instead of prescribed contact points. This paper presents the planner, implemented through virtual model control, and its evaluation on a Shadow Dexterous Hand. Without feedback, the planned closing motions tolerated size errors of about 1cm and pose errors of several centimeters and tens of degrees, a wider range than a state-of-the-art data-driven planner in 25 of 27 tested conditions. They also grasped 82.5% of 80 everyday objects and succeeded within an autonomous pipeline. Robustness can thus be designed into the closing motion itself, rather than left only to feedback. This planner opens a path toward reliable manipulation in uncertain settings, which we will pursue by combining it with adaptive feedback control on the physical hand.

</details>


### [107] [Learning Safe Humanoid Navigation from Reduced Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19272)
*William D. Compton,Zachary Olkin,Ryan Bena,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoM-Nav两阶段导航框架：先在降阶动力学上用3D LiDAR训练导航策略，再迁移到全阶人形动力学（冻结运动策略），并加Poisson安全滤波，实现Unitree G1的无地图多楼层导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段RL导航流水线难以扩展到多层级、多楼层地形，瓶颈在于人形机器人与复杂地形（如楼梯）交互困难。

Method: 将导航问题分解为两步：1）在降阶动力学模型上、使用完整3D LiDAR观测训练复杂多层地形导航策略；2）以该导航知识启动全阶人形动力学策略，循环中冻结运动（locomotion）策略。另在导航策略输出上施加Poisson安全滤波以应对分布外障碍。

Result: 在Unitree G1上验证RoM-Nav，完成无地图多层导航，单次试验垂直位移超过10m、路径长度超过100m；Poisson安全滤波在分布外障碍下恢复安全性且不降低导航成功率。

Conclusion: 两阶段分解+策略迁移+安全滤波可有效实现人形机器人复杂多层地形的无地图长距离导航。

Abstract: Research in humanoid robotics has achieved rapid progress in locomotion, and recent results have pushed the boundary on autonomous navigation. We demonstrate that a standard single-stage RL navigation pipeline struggles to scale to multi-level and multi-story terrain, limited by the difficulty of complex humanoid terrain interactions such as stairs. To overcome this challenge, we decompose the navigation problem into two pieces. First, we train a policy operating on the reduced order dynamics but with full 3D LiDAR observations to navigate complex, multi-story terrain. We then utilize this navigation knowledge to kickstart a policy operating on the full-order humanoid dynamics, with a frozen locomotion policy in the loop. Additionally, we demonstrate that applying a Poisson safety filter to the navigation policy output recovers safety in the presence of out-of-distribution obstacles, without dropping navigation success rate. We demonstrate the resulting RoM-Nav policy on a Unitree G1, accomplishing mapless multi-floor navigation covering trials with over 10m of vertical displacement and over 100m of path length. Project page with videos https://wdc3iii.github.io/rom-nav/ .

</details>


### [108] [OHRID-Retail: An Open Multimodal Dataset of Human Activity in Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19302)
*Xiangrui Wang,Yuetong Wu,Jalen Beeman,Robert Cook,Yu Gu,Nathanial Pearson,Trevor Smith,Read Hayes,Boyi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布了OHRID Retail——一个面向零售场景的开放式人体多模态数据集，采集16名健康成年人分别在无机器人、低速机器人引导与高速机器人引导三种条件下完成模拟货架取物任务时的全身运动学与肌电信号，以支持人机交互、生物力学与行为识别等研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述人类在与移动机器人共享环境中的行为数据集仍然有限，尤其缺乏结合移动、伸手、物体操作与机器人引导运动的零售活动数据。

Method: 招募16名健康成年人，在三种被试内条件（无机器人、低速机器人引导、高速机器人引导）下执行模拟货架取物任务，每条件每人完成两次试验。使用17个Xsens Awinda惯性传感器采集全身运动学，使用Delsys Trigno表面肌电传感器在10个部位测量肌肉活动，并进行描述性分析。

Result: 描述性分析显示，全身运动强度与肌肉激活在不同机器人交互条件和不同身体部位之间存在差异；数据集公开提供原始记录、处理后指标、文档与可复现分析资源。

Conclusion: OHRID Retail可支撑人类活动识别、多模态传感器融合、职业生物力学、人因工程、人本机器人导航以及零售及相关共享环境中的人机交互等研究。

Abstract: Open datasets describing human behavior in environments shared with mobile robots remain limited, particularly for retail activities that combine locomotion, reaching, object handling, and robot guided movement. This paper introduces OHRID Retail, an open, human centered multimodal dataset collected from 16 healthy adults performing a simulated shelf picking task under three within participant conditions: no robot, low speed robot guidance, and high speed robot guidance. Each participant completed two trials per condition. Whole body kinematics were recorded using 17 Xsens Awinda inertial sensors and muscle activity was measured at 10 locations using Delsys Trigno surface electromyography sensors. Descriptive analyses demonstrate variation in whole body movement intensity and muscle activation across robot interaction conditions and body locations. OHRID Retail provides openly available raw recordings, processed measures, documentation, and reproducible analysis resources. The dataset can support research in human activity recognition, multimodal sensor fusion, occupational biomechanics, ergonomics, human aware robot navigation, and human robot interaction in retail and related shared environments.

</details>


### [109] [GAVEL: Graph World Models for Verified and Efficient Long-Horizon LLM Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19315)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Swarajh Mehta,Jiwoo Kim,Zhihao Dou,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAVEL：基于显式图世界模型的LLM长时程规划验证与修复框架，在BEHAVIOR-1K上大幅提升单/多任务成功率并降低搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽为长时程机器人规划提供灵活接口，但生成计划常违反本体（embodiment）约束、难以从规划错误中恢复、且在部分可观测下推理不佳。

Method: 构建显式图世界模型，编码物体关系、动作前置条件与效果、以及未观测物体位置的概率信念；在执行前预测动作后果、检测违规并修复可由世界模型直接推出的错误，仅将需语义推理的错误交回LLM重规划；对多任务指令，基于物体位置分布重排剩余子任务以最小化期望搜索代价。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K的100个单长时程任务与500条多任务指令上评测；使用Qwen3-8B时，单任务成功率由41.2%升至91.8%，多任务由19.9%升至92.6%；分布信念推理相比静态变体减少约5.4%的移动距离。

Conclusion: 显式图世界模型封装可显著提升长时程具身规划的可靠性与效率，且对紧凑型与前沿托管LLM均有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for long-horizon robot planning, but generated plans often fail to respect embodiment constraints, recover from planning errors, or reason effectively under partial observability. We present GAVEL, a framework for verifying and repairing long-horizon LLM planning built around an explicit graph world model. The graph represents relevant object-relations, action pre-conditions and effects, and probabilistic beliefs over unobserved object locations. This model can predict the consequences of LLM-generated actions before execution, detect violations, and repair those whose corrections follow directly from the world model. This method also reserves LLM replanning solely for errors requiring semantic reasoning. For multi-task instructions, GAVEL reasons over distributions of possible object locations to reorder remaining subtasks and minimize expected search cost. We evaluate GAVEL on BEHAVIOR-1K across 100 single long-horizon tasks and 500 multi-task instructions. With Qwen3-8B, GAVEL improves single-task success from 41.2% to 91.8% and multi-task success from 19.9% to 92.6%. Distributional belief reasoning also reduces travel distance by approximately 5.4% compared with a static variant. These improvements show that an explicit graph world model harness can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon embodied planning across compact and frontier hosted LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [110] [A Morphing Aerial Robot With Thruster-Integrated Flexible Continuum Links for Shape Adaptive Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19328)
*Eri Sawada,Kazuki Sugihara,Ayano Miyamichi,Kunio Kojima,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带柔性连续体连杆的变形式分布式旋翼空中机器人，同时实现高形状适应性与扩展的力旋量空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操作是拓展飞行机器人应用的关键。现有研究主要有两个方向：(i) 用柔性/软机械臂获得高环境适应性；(ii) 将推进器沿机械臂分布以扩展可行力旋量空间。但尚无空中机器人能同时满足这两项要求。

Method: 设计变形式旋翼分布式空中机器人，采用柔性连续体连杆：连杆作为软机械臂被动顺应环境形状；沿连杆分布的推进器扩大可行推力旋量空间，使末端执行器能施加较大交互力。为抑制轻质连续体连杆的振动，开发兼具高扭转与垂直刚度的复合板簧结构，并提出抑振控制方法。

Result: 在所提实现方案下，实现了稳定飞行及多种空中操作任务。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个用集成推进器的柔性连杆实现空中操作的工作，同时兼顾形状适应性与扩展力旋量空间。

Abstract: In recent years, aerial manipulation has attracted increasing attention as a key to expand the application of aerial robots. In this work, we focus on two major research directions for achieving versatile aerial manipulation: (i) acquiring high environmental adaptability using soft manipulators, and (ii) expanding the feasible wrench space by distributing thrusters along the manipulator. However, no aerial robot has simultaneously satisfied these two requirements. Therefore, in this paper, we propose a morphing rotor-distributed aerial robot with flexible continuum links that achieves both high shape adaptability and an expanded wrench space. The flexible continuum links function as soft manipulators, passively conforming to the shape of the environment, while the thrusters distributed along the continuum links expand the feasible thrust wrench space and enable the end-effector to exert large interaction forces. To realize the proposed robot, it is essential to suppress vibrations of the lightweight continuum links. Thus, we develop a composite leaf-spring structure that provides both high torsional and vertical stiffness, and vibration-suppressing control methods. Using these implementations, we demonstrate stable flight and a variety of aerial manipulation tasks. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to realize aerial manipulations using flexible links with an integrated thruster.

</details>


### [111] [SemSafe-3DGS: Semantic Risk-Aware Active Navigation in Uncertain 3D Gaussian Splatting Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19330)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义风险感知的安全主动感知导航框架，在带属性3D高斯地图中通过类别风险权重调制AVaR碰撞间隙模型，并将加权间隙聚合为CBF，与主动感知屏障统一集成到CBF-QP中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全规划主要基于几何推理，忽略语义差异，导致几何相似但语义后果不同的场景元素产生相近控制响应，安全性不足。

Method: 面向带属性3D高斯地图，用语义属性通过类别相关风险权重调制Average Value-at-Risk碰撞间隙模型，使安全关键高斯基元在复合屏障中权重更大；将加权间隙聚合为控制屏障函数，并设计轨迹相关的主动感知屏障以降低预期运动路径上的几何地图不确定性；两者统一于CBF-QP，将语义风险感知避碰作为硬约束，在信息获取与安全或任务冲突时可松弛。

Result: 实验验证了高效的安全约束、主动感知带来的导航性能提升、语义相关的轨迹自适应，以及在Ackermann动力学下的真机执行。

Conclusion: 语义风险信息可有效融入安全主动感知导航，实现兼顾安全约束与信息获取的实时控制，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in partially observed environments must navigate safely while acquiring observations that improve future planning. Existing safety formulations generally reason primarily about geometry. Consequently, geometrically similar scene elements may induce comparable control responses despite having different semantic consequences. We present a semantic risk aware safe-active perception framework for navigation in attributed 3D Gaussian maps. Semantic attributes modulate an Average Value-at-Risk collision clearance model through class dependent risk weights, allowing safety-critical Gaussian primitives to receive greater influence in the composite barrier. The resulting weighted clearances are aggregated into a control barrier function, while a trajectory-relevant active perception barrier promotes observations that reduce geometric map uncertainty along the robot's anticipated motion. Both objectives are integrated in a unified CBF-QP that enforces semantic risk-aware collision avoidance as a hard constraint while relaxing information acquisition when it conflicts with safety or task progress. Experiments demonstrate efficient safety constraint, improved navigation through active perception, semantic dependent trajectory adaptation, and real-robot execution under Ackermann dynamics.

</details>


### [112] [Dynamic-LIVO: A Dynamic-Aware LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry System Using Spatio-Temporal Normals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19336)
*Zhixin Zhang,Samuel Ahiwe,Matthew Hale,Liang Zhao,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic-LIVO，一种动态感知的LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计系统，用于动态环境下的鲁棒状态估计与静态彩色建图，核心是时空法线分析与时延估计策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中，动态物体的LiDAR测量及其关联的视觉观测会污染状态估计与建图。现有方法难以在动态场景下同时保证定位鲁棒性与地图的静态性。时空法线分析在新观测或空间稀疏区域因观测不足而分类不可靠。

Method: 1) 采用时空（S-T）法线分析识别动态LiDAR点，并将分类结果传播到LiDAR-惯性与视觉-惯性两个更新环节，阻止动态测量参与状态估计与建图；2) 引入时延S-T法线估计策略，对约束不足的点暂缓分类，待更多观测积累后重新评估，从而提升动态分类可靠性并保留有效静态点用于建图。

Result: 在公开数据集与自采集数据集（多种传感器配置）上的大量实验表明，Dynamic-LIVO在具有挑战性的动态环境中提升了定位精度，并生成更干净的静态彩色地图。

Conclusion: 时空法线分析结合时延重评估策略可有效提升动态场景下的动态点分类可靠性，实现鲁棒状态估计与静态彩色建图；源码与自采集数据集将在录用后公开。

Abstract: This paper proposes Dynamic-LIVO, a dynamic-aware LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) system for robust state estimation and static colored mapping in dynamic environments. Dynamic-LIVO employs Spatio-Temporal (S-T) normal analysis to identify dynamic LiDAR points and propagates the resulting classification to both LiDAR-inertial and visual-inertial updates, preventing dynamic LiDAR measurements and their associated visual observations from affecting state estimation and mapping. However, S-T normal estimation can be unreliable in newly observed and spatially sparse regions due to insufficient spatio-temporal observations. To address this issue, we introduce a time-delayed S-T normal estimation strategy that defers the classification of insufficiently constrained points and re-evaluates them as additional observations become available. This strategy improves dynamic classification reliability while preserving valid static points for map construction. Extensive experiments on public and self-collected datasets with diverse sensor configurations demonstrate that Dynamic-LIVO improves localization accuracy and produces cleaner static colored maps in challenging dynamic environments. The source code and self-collected dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [113] [ViLoMan: Learning Visual-Proprioceptive Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation Skills for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19340)
*Zejie Tian,Ruibing Hou,Bingpeng Ma,Börje F. Karlsson,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViLoMan：将不完整的人-物交互运动学演示补全为可物理执行的机器人轨迹，并通过教师-学生蒸馏学习统一全身控制策略，实现仅凭机载深度与本体感知的自主人形loco-manipulation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形loco-manipulation需要自适应的全身协调以融合移动与物理交互。当前挑战在于：缺乏多样且可物理执行的机器人-物体交互数据，且难以直接从机载观测学习统一的全身控制策略。

Method: ViLoMan框架分两步：1）将人-物交互的部分运动学演示转化为完整、可物理执行的机器人轨迹；2）在这些轨迹上采用教师-学生蒸馏，学习将自我中心深度观测与本体感知直接映射为关节级全身动作的统一策略。部署时无需参考运动或中间指令。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界中，对多种门配置及不同机器人初始条件的关门任务进行评估。单个策略使Unitree G1仅用机载深度感知与本体感知完成完整任务，并在任务变化下稳健泛化，且有效实现sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: ViLoMan提供了一种可扩展的自主人形loco-manipulation方案，通过演示补全与统一策略蒸馏，摆脱对参考运动与中间指令的依赖，展示出良好的泛化性与实机迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires adaptive whole-body coordination to seamlessly integrate locomotion and physical interaction. Despite recent advances, learning autonomous loco-manipulation remains challenging due to the scarcity of diverse, physically executable robot-object interaction data and the difficulty of learning unified whole-body control directly from onboard observations. We present ViLoMan, a scalable framework for autonomous humanoid loco-manipulation. ViLoMan first transforms partial kinematic demonstrations of human-object interactions into complete, physically executable robot trajectories. It then leverages these trajectories within a teacher-student distillation framework to learn a unified policy that maps egocentric depth observations and proprioceptive measurements directly to joint-level whole-body actions. During deployment, the policy requires neither reference motions nor intermediate commands. We evaluate ViLoMan on door-closing tasks across diverse door configurations and robot initial conditions in both simulation and the real world. Experimental results demonstrate that a single policy enables a Unitree G1 humanoid to complete the full task using only onboard depth sensing and proprioception, while generalizing robustly across task variations and transferring effectively from simulation to reality. Project page: viloman-anonymous.pages.dev.

</details>


### [114] [Kinematics-Grounded Agentic AI for Robotic Additive Manufacturing Process Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19347)
*Jingzhan Ge,Ruimin Chen,Azadeh Haghighi,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出A-RAM代理式机器人增材制造框架，将用户意图与零件文件转化为可追溯、可执行的规划方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人增材制造使工艺规划依赖机器人本体：切片决策与零件朝向决定生成路径，而零件朝向与工作空间放置又影响其运动学可实现性，导致在零件坐标系中看似有利的切片方案在机械臂上可能不可行或不利。现有AM工具、基于LLM的决策支持方法与数字孪生系统均无法对上述耦合决策进行集成的执行前评估。

Method: A-RAM采用"代理—专家—工具"框架：LLM解读制造目标与约束，识别既定变量与可搜索规划变量，并以模式约束的请求编码推理；确定性规划代理实例化相应搜索流程；领域工具计算切片、放置、逆运动学、轨迹时序、Joint-6加加速度与挤出等定量证据。

Result: 在六轴机械臂AM单元上通过三个案例研究评估，涵盖专家指定规划、仅目标规划、目标相关填充筛选与几何相关朝向—放置选择。所选方案相比最不利的有效候选，最大Joint-6加加速度降低达53.5%，平均绝对Joint-6加加速度降低达48.3%；目标特定填充筛选使运动规划完成时间缩短达40.1%，挤出路径缩短达12.7%。

Conclusion: A-RAM实现了对切片—朝向—放置耦合决策的集成式执行前评估，可将用户意图与零件文件转化为可追溯、可执行的机器人增材制造规划，并显著改善运动学与挤出性能。

Abstract: Robotic additive manufacturing (AM) extends material-extrusion printing beyond gantry kinematics but makes process planning robot-dependent. A slicer-generated plan that appears favorable in part coordinates can become infeasible or robotically unfavorable on a manipulator because slicer-process decisions and part orientation determine the generated path, while part orientation and workspace placement affect its kinematic realization. Existing AM tools, large language model (LLM)-based decision-support methods, and digital-shadow systems do not provide integrated pre-execution evaluation of these coupled decisions. This paper presents agentic robotic additive manufacturing (A-RAM), an agent-specialist-tool framework that converts user intent and a part file into traceable, execution-ready plans. The LLM interprets manufacturing objectives and constraints, identifies prescribed and searchable planning variables, and encodes this reasoning in a schema-constrained request; a deterministic Planning Agent instantiates the corresponding search workflow, while domain tools compute quantitative evidence for slicing, placement, inverse kinematics, trajectory timing, Joint-6 jerk, and extrusion. The framework is evaluated on a six-axis robotic-arm AM cell through three case studies covering expert-specified planning, goal-only planning, objective-dependent infill screening, and geometry-dependent orientation-placement selection. Across the evaluated candidate sets, selected plans achieve up to 53.5% lower maximum Joint-6 jerk and 48.3% lower mean absolute Joint-6 jerk than the least favorable valid candidates, while objective-specific infill screening yields motion-plan completion times up to 40.1% shorter and extrusion paths up to 12.7% shorter than the corresponding least favorable screened patterns.

</details>


### [115] [Design and Experimental Validation of a 3D Printed Torsional Series Elastic Actuator for Safe Human Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19367)
*Joel Hidalgo Pisco,Melissa Cobos Condo,Luigi Miranda,Dennys Paillacho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计、有限元分析并实验验证了一种基于3D打印热塑性聚氨酯(TPU)的低刚度扭转弹簧,用于串联弹性驱动器(SEA),并配合带力矩阈值切换的混合位置控制器,实现安全、低成本的物理人机交互(pHRI)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机交互(pHRI)中内在安全是社交与服务机器人的关键要求。串联弹性驱动器(SEA)虽提供基于硬件的柔顺性,但传统金属设计往往需要复杂的多部件装配,因此需要一种更简单、低成本且易制造的柔顺弹性元件。

Method: 采用3D打印TPU制造低刚度扭转弹簧作为SEA柔顺元件;进行有限元分析(FEA)预测其力学性能;并构建带力矩阈值切换的混合位置控制器,以配合标准的位置限制型伺服电机应对外部交互;通过实验评估轨迹跟踪与柔顺屈服能力。

Result: 弹簧呈现高度线性的力矩-变形响应(Ks = 0.066 Nm/degree),与数值预测偏差小于3%,差异归因于FDM结构各向异性。系统能准确跟踪非平稳轨迹并在外部扰动下安全屈服;TPU固有材料阻尼起到被动低通滤波作用,抑制控制模式切换时的高频振荡。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构为安全的pHRI提供了一种低成本、可靠且易于制造的解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring intrinsic safety in physical human robot interaction (pHRI) is a critical requirement for social and service robots. While Series Elastic Actuators (SEAs) offer hardware based compliance, traditional metallic designs often require complex, multi part assemblies. This paper presents the design, finite element analysis (FEA), and experimental validation of a low stiffness, torsional spring for SEAs, manufactured via 3D printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). The compliant element exhibits a highly linear torque deformation response (Ks = 0.066 Nm/degree), matching numerical predictions with under 3% deviation, a variance attributed to FDM structural anisotropy. To accommodate external interactions using standard position limited servomotors, a hybrid position controller with torque threshold switching was implemented. Experimental evaluations demonstrate the system ability to accurately track non stationary trajectories and safely yield to external disturbances. Furthermore, the inherent material damping of the TPU acts as a passive low pass filter, preventing high frequency oscillations during control mode transitions. The proposed architecture offers a cost effective, reliable, and easily manufacturable solution for safe pHRI.

</details>


### [116] [Enhancing the Perception of Safety and Comfort during Physical Human-Robot Handshake Interactions by Integrating Flexible Elements into a Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19375)
*Joel Hidalgo,Dennys Paillacho,Melissa Cobos,Luigi Miranda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验比较刚性关节与串联弹性驱动器（SEA）机械臂在握手交互中的效果，结果显示SEA能提升用户感知的安全性与舒适度，支持其在人机物理交互中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机物理交互中安全与舒适至关重要，而握手等自然手势因直接物理接触成为难点；采用串联弹性驱动器（SEA）吸收冲击是提升交互安全性的设计策略，但缺乏对其影响感知安全与交互体验的实证评估。

Method: 10名志愿者（6男4女）分别与两种机械臂各完成一次握手：一种为刚性关节，一种为采用SEA的柔性关节。实验中采集末端执行器轨迹的客观数据，并通过聚焦安全、自然性和信心的感知问卷收集主观数据，实现刚性与弹性元素效果的直接对比。

Result: 问卷结果显示，配备SEA的机械臂在安全性和舒适性方面获得更高的感知评分，支持其促进更安全、更具社会接受度的人机交互。

Conclusion: 在机械臂中引入串联弹性驱动器可有效提升握手交互中用户感知的安全与舒适，表明SEA有潜力改善机器人系统在日常场景中的物理安全性与社会接受度。

Abstract: Safety and comfort in human-robot physical in-teractions are essential aspects in the development of social technologies, where natural gestures, such as handshakes, represent a challenge due to their direct physical contact. The implementation of series elastic actuators (SEA) to absorb impacts is proposed as a design strategy that favors safer interactions. This paper presents an experimental study aimed at evaluating how the incorporation of SEAs in robotic arms influences perceived safety and the interaction experience dur-ing handshaking. The design allows a direct comparison of the effect of rigidity versus the incorporation of elastic elements, in order to identify the advantages of SEAs in improving the physical safety and social acceptance of robotic systems in everyday contexts. The experiment was carried out with 10 volunteers (6 men and 4 women), who performed two interactions with each robotic arm: one with rigid joints and the other with flexible joints using SEA. During testing, objective data on end-effector trajectories were collected, as well as subjective information through a perception survey focused on safety, naturalness, and confidence during the handshake. The survey results show increased perceptions of safety and comfort with the SEA-equipped arm, supporting its potential to facilitate safer and more socially accepted human-robot interactions.

</details>


### [117] [Task-Oriented Active Learning of Residual Dynamics for Model Predictive Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19378)
*Nobuaki Aoki,Hojin Lee,Stefan Sosnowski,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Task-Oriented Information Acquisition (ToIA)，一种面向任务的主动学习准则，用于结合在线高斯过程残差学习的MPPI控制。ToIA在现有rollout批次上评估观测对后续预测不确定性的削减，并以任务相关性加权，在越野导航仿真中将目标到达成功率较被动GP学习提升19.3与27.4个百分点，并优于任务无关主动学习基线，支持20 Hz在线控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线残差学习可降低预测控制中的模型失配，但被动数据采集难以覆盖任务后期才变得重要的状态；而任务无关的主动学习虽针对不确定或信息量大的区域，这些区域获取的信息未必能提升任务性能。因此需要一种将信息采集与任务目标对齐的准则。

Method: ToIA针对带在线GP残差学习的MPPI控制：对每个采样的控制序列，估计在rollout早期获得的观测能在多大程度上降低同一rollout后续状态的预测不确定性，并以该rollout对任务的相关性对该削减量加权。评分直接在现有MPPI rollout批次上计算，无需采样未来观测，也无需在假设后验更新下重新优化控制。

Result: 在异构地形、留出地图的越野导航仿真中，ToIA相比被动GP学习将目标到达成功率提升19.3与27.4个百分点，并在密集与稀疏在线学习间隔下均优于任务无关主动学习基线。消融研究表明，在稀疏模型更新下任务相关性尤为重要。实现可在NVIDIA RTX 2080 Ti上以20 Hz进行在线控制。

Conclusion: 将信息获取与任务相关性对齐的主动学习准则能有效提升在线残差学习MPPI的控制性能，尤其在模型更新稀疏时收益显著，且计算开销可满足实时控制需求。

Abstract: Online residual learning can reduce model mismatch in predictive control, but passive data collection may fail to adequately cover states that become important later in the task. Task-agnostic active learning targets uncertain or informative regions, but information acquired in such regions does not necessarily improve task performance. This paper introduces Task-Oriented Information Acquisition (ToIA), an active-learning criterion for model predictive path integral control (MPPI) with online Gaussian process (GP) residual learning. For each sampled control sequence, ToIA estimates how much an observation obtained early in the rollout would reduce predictive uncertainty at later states on the same rollout, and weights this reduction by the rollout's relevance to the task. The score is evaluated over the existing MPPI rollout batch without sampling future observations or re-optimizing control under hypothetical posterior updates. In simulated off-road navigation across held-out maps with heterogeneous terrain, ToIA improved the goal-reaching success rate over passive GP learning by 19.3 and 27.4 percentage points and outperformed task-agnostic active-learning baselines across dense and sparse online-learning intervals. An ablation study indicates that task relevance is particularly important under sparse model updates. The implementation supports online control at 20 Hz on an NVIDIA RTX 2080 Ti.

</details>


### [118] [From Rollout to Reset: A Graph-Based Harness for Autonomous Long-Horizon Manipulation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19413)
*Jing Jiang,Yue Yang,Xinkai Jiang,Gedas Bertasius,Daniel J. Szafir,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HALTER，一个面向长时程机器人操作任务的自主评估与场景重置框架，通过学习到的原子重置技能库规划恢复场景，替代人工重置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人评估仍依赖人工重置场景，耗费操作员时间，且初始状态分布未加规定，导致结果难以复现。现有自动化系统 AutoEval 仅适用于单步任务，因为长时程 rollout 的终止状态组合爆炸，单一学习式重置策略无法覆盖。

Method: HALTER 在线构建空间场景图（点云 + 视觉基础模型），由 LLM 在该图上推理，完成 rollout 评分、重置规划与重置成功验证；重置通过在原子重置技能库上规划实现，因此示范成本随技能库规模而非终止状态数增长，且无需采集任何任务的成功标注图像。

Result: 在 Franka 机械臂的四个长时程任务上：场景恢复成功率 76%（AutoEval 52%，运动规划式重置 65%）；已完成技能比例估计正确率 90%（对比 76%）；重置验证判定正确率 91%（AutoEval 78%）；评估流程的操作员时间相对人工重置减少 72%。在三个留出任务上的组合泛化中，HALTER 重置成功率 74.7%，而按任务训练的重置策略仅 1.3%。另对场景表示与图更新频率进行了消融。

Conclusion: 通过技能库规划式重置与 LLM 驱动的场景图推理，HALTER 将长时程任务的自主评估与重置扩展到免人工、免标注且可组合泛化的水平，显著优于单步自动化方案与运动规划基线。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are improving quickly, and real-robot evaluation remains the standard evidence for that progress. It still relies on a human to reset the scene between rollouts, which consumes operator time and leaves the initial state distribution unspecified, so results reproduce poorly. A recent system, AutoEval, automates both reset and scoring, but only for single-step tasks, because a long-horizon rollout can terminate in combinatorially many configurations that no single learned reset policy covers. We present HALTER, a Harness for Autonomous Long-horizon Task Evaluation and Reset, which restores the scene by planning over a library of learned atomic reset skills, so demonstration cost scales with the size of that library rather than with the number of terminal states. HALTER builds a spatial scene graph online from point clouds and vision foundation models, and an LLM reasons over this graph to score the rollout, plan the reset, and verify that the reset succeeded, without collecting labeled success images for any task. On four long-horizon tasks on a Franka arm, HALTER restores the scene in 76% of episodes, against 52% for AutoEval and 65% for a motion-planning reset, and it estimates the completed-skill fraction correctly in 90% of episodes, against 76%.
